1a1(rgyud): @//po ti 'di la 'phags pa 'jam dpal phan yon dang dus 'khor rtsa rgyud 
1a2(rgyud): sogs bzhugs/
2a1(rgyud): @##/ O /Ar+ya man ny+dzu shrI 
2a2(rgyud): na ma sang gi ti/bod skad du/
2b1(rgyud): 'phags pa 'jam dpal gyi mtshan yang dag par brjod pa 
2b2(rgyud): 'phags pa 'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pa la phyag 'tshalo/
2b3(rgyud): de nas dpal ldan rdo rje 'chang //gdul dka' 'dul ba 
3a1(rgyud): rnams kyi mchog/dpa' bo 'jig 
3a2(rgyud): rten gsum las rgyal//rdo rje dbang phyug bsang ba'i rgyal/
3a3(rgyud): pad ma dkar po rgyas 'dra'i spyan//pad ma rgyas 'dra'i 
3b1(rgyud): zhal mnga' ba//rang gi lag gi rdo rje mchog/yang dang yang du gsor byed pa/
3b2(rgyud): khro gnyer rim pa ldan la sogs//lag na rdo rje mtha' yas pa//dpa' bo 
3b3(rgyud): gdul dka' 'dul ba po//'jigs su rung dang dpa' byad can//rdo rje 
3b4(rgyud): rtso  mo rab 'phro ba//rang gi lag gi gsor byed pa//snying rje che dang shes 
4a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rab dang //thabs kyis 'gro don byed pa'i mchog/
4a2(rgyud): dga' mgu rangs pa'i bsams pa can//khro bo lus kyi gzugs ldan pa/
4a3(rgyud): sangs rgyas 'phrin las byed pa'i mgon//lus btud rnams dang lhan    
4a4(rgyud): cig du//mgon po bcom ldan de bzhin gshegs//rdzogs sangs
4b1(rgyud): rgyas la phyag 'tshal nas//thal mo sbyar bar byas nas ni//spyan sngar 'dug ste 'di skad gsol//khyab bdag 
4b2(rgyud): bdag la sman pa dang //bdag don bdag la thugs brtse'i phyir//sgyu 'phrul dra bas mngon rdzogs pa'i//byang 
4b3(rgyud): chub ci nas bdag thob mdzod//nyon mongs pas ni sems dkrugs shing //mi shes 'dam du byin ba yi//sems 
4b4(rgyud): can kun la sman pa dang //bla med 'bras bu thob bya'i phyir//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das//'gro 
4b5(rgyud): ba'i (ba)ma ston pa po//dam tshig chen po de nyid mkhyen//dbang po bsam pa mkhyen mchog gis//bcom 
5a1(rgyud): ldan 'das kyis ye shes sku//gtsug gtor chen po tshig gi bdag/ye shes sku 
5a2(rgyud): ste rang byung ba//'jam dpal ye shes sems dpa' yi//ming ni yang dag mchog/don zab don ni rgya che zhing //
5a3(rgyud): don chen mtshungs med rab zhi ba//thog ma bar dang mthar dge ba//'das pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyis 
5a4(rgyud): gsungs//ma 'ongs rnams kyang gsung 'gyur la//da ltar byung ba rdzogs sangs rgyas//yang dang yang du 
5a5(rgyud): gsung ba gang //rgyud chen sgyu 'phrul dra ba las//rdo rje 'chang chen gsang sngags 'chang //dpag med 
5b1(rgyud): rnams kyi dka' bzhin du//glur blangs gang lags bshad du gsol//mgon po rdzogs sangs rgyas kun gyi//gsang 'dzin ci 
5b2(rgyud): nas bdag 'gyur phyir//nges par 'byung gi bar du 'di//bdag gi bsam pa brtan pos bzung //nyon mongs ma lus bsal ba dang //
5b3(rgyud): mi shes ma lus spang ba'i phyir//bsam pa'i khyad par ji bzhin du//sems can rnams la bshad par 'tshal//gsang dbang lag na rdo rje 
5b4(rgyud): yis//de bzhin gshegs la de skad du//gsol nas thal mo sbyar byas te//lus btud nas ni spyan sngar 'dug/zhus pa'i le'u ste 
5b5(rgyud): dang po'o////de nas bcom ldan shAkya thub//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rkang gnyis mchog/nyid kyi zhal nas ljags bzang 
5b6(rgyud): ba//ring zhing yangs pa brkyang mdzad de//'jig rten gsum po snang byed cing //bdud bzhi'i dgra rnams 'dul byed pa//sems can 
6a1(rgyud): rnams kyi ngan song gsum//sbyong bar byed pa'i 'dzum bstan nas//tshangs pa'i gsungs ni snyen pa yis//
6a2(rgyud): 'jig rten gsum po kun bkang nas//lag na rdo rje stobs po che//gsang dbang la ni bslar gsungs pa//snying rje che dang ldan gyur pas//'gro 
6a3(rgyud): la phan pa'i don du khyod//ye shes lus can 'jam dpal gyi//ming brjod pas ni don che ba//dag par byed cing sdig sel ba//nga las nyan 
6a4(rgyud): par brtson pa ni//legs so dpal ldan rdo rje 'chang //lag na rdo rje khyod legs so//gsang ba'i bdag po de phyir ngas//khyod la legs par 
6a5(rgyud): bstan par bya//khyod ni rtse gcig yid kyis nyon//bcom ldan de ni legs zhes gsol//lan slar gsungs pa'i le'u ste gnyis pa'o//
6a6(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan shAkya thub//gsang sngags rigs chen thams cad dang //gsang sngags rigs sngags 'chang ba'i rigs//rigs 
6b1(rgyud): gsum la ni rnam par gzigs//'jig rten 'jig rten 'das pa'i rigs//'jig rten snang byed rigs chen dang //phyag rgya chen po'i rigs mchog dang //
6b2(rgyud): rin chen gtsug gtor cher gzigs nas//rigs drugs la gzigs pa'i le'u ste gsum pa'o////tshigs gi bdag po tshigs su bcad/
6b3(rgyud): gsang sngags rgyal po drug ldan zhing //gnyis su med par 'byung ba dang //mi skye chos can 'di gsung pa////a A/i I/u U/e ai/o 
6b4(rgyud): au/aM A/s+thi to hri ti/dz+nyA na mur ti ra haM bud to//bud d+ha naM/t+yad ta bar ti naM/A+oM badzra tig Sh+Ta/du kha tshe da/prad dz+nyA dz+nyA na mur ta ye/dz+nyA na ka ya/
6b5(rgyud): ba gi shwa ra/a ra pa tsa na ya te na ma////sgyu 'phrul dra ba mngon par byang chub pa'i le'u ste bzhi pa'o////'di ltar sangs rgyas bcom 
6b6(rgyud): ldan 'das//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas a las byung //a ni yig 'bru kun gyi mchog//don chen yi ge dam pa yin//khong nas byung ba skye ba med//tshig
7a1(rgyud): tu brjod pa spangs pa ste//brjod pa kun gyi rgyu yi mchog/tshig kun rab tu gsal bar byed//mchod pa chen po 'dod chags 
7a2(rgyud): pa che//sems can thams cad dga' bar byed//mchod pa chenapo zhe sdang che//nyon mongs kun gyi dgra che ba//mchod pa chen po gti mug che//gti mug blo ste 
7a3(rgyud): gti mug sel//mchod pa chen po khro ba che//khro ba chen po dgra che ba//mchod pa che po chags pa che//chags pa thams cad sel bar byed//'dod pa chen po 
7a4(rgyud): bde ba che//dga' ba chen po mgu ba che//gzugs che lus kyang che ba ste//kha dog che zhing lus bongs che//ming yang che zhing rgya che ba//dkyil 'khor chen 
7a5(rgyud): po yangs pa yin//shes rab mtshon chen 'chang ba ste//nyon mongs chen po lcags kyu mchog/grags chen snyan grags chen po ste//snang ba chen po gsal 
7a6(rgyud): ba che//mkhas pa sgyu 'phrul chen po 'chang //sgyu 'phrul chen po'i don grub pa//sgyu 'phrul chen po'i dga' bas dga'//sgyu 'phrul chen po mig 'phrul 
7a7(rgyud): can//sbyin bdag chen po gtso bo ste//tshul khrims chen po 'chang ba'i mchog/bzod chen 'chang ba bsten pa po//brtson 'grus chen po brtul ba yin//
7b1(rgyud): bsam gtan chen po'i ting 'dzin gnas//shes rab chen po'i lus 'chang ba//stobs po che la thabs che ba//smon lam ye shes rgya mtsho ste//byams chen rang bzhin 
7b2(rgyud): dpag tu med//snying rje chen po blo'i mchog/shes rab chen po blo chen ldan//mkhas pa chen po thabs che ba//rdzu 'phrul chen po stobs dang ldan//shugs chen 
7b3(rgyud): 'gyogs pa chen po ste//rdzu 'phrul chen po cher grags pa//stobs chen pha rol gnon pa po//srid pa'i ri bo chen po 'joms//mkhregs shing rdo rje chen po 'chang //
7b4(rgyud): drag po chen po drag shul che//'jigs chen 'jigs par byed pa po//mgon po rigs mchog chen po ste//bla ma gsang sngags che ba'i mchog/theg pa chen po'i tshul 
7b5(rgyud): la gnas//theg pa chen po'i tshul gyi mchog/byang sems rdo rje'i lnga pa'o////sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad che//thub pa chen po thub 
7b6(rgyud): chen ldan//gsang sngags tshul chen las byung ba//gsang sngags tshul chen bdag nyid can//pha rol phyin bcu thob pa ste//pha rol phyin bcu la gnas//pha rol 
7b7(rgyud): phyin bcu dag pha ste//pha rol phyin pa bcu'i tshul//mgon po sa bcu'i dbang phyug ste//sa bcu la ni gnas pa po//shes bcu rnam dag bdag nyid can//shes bcu 
8a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rnam dag 'chang ba po//rnam pa bcu po don bcu'i don//thu dbang stobs bcu khyab pa'i bdag//kun gyi don ni ma lus byed//rnam 
8a2(rgyud): bcu dbang ldan che ba po//thog ma med pa spros med bdag//de bzhin nyid bdag dag pa'i bdag//bden par smra zhing tshig mi 'gyur//de skad smra pa de bzhin byed //gnyis 
8a3(rgyud): su med dang gnyis med ston//yang dag mtha' las rnam par gnas//bdag med seng ge'i sgra dang ldan //mu stegs ri dgas ngan 'jigs byed/kun tu 'gro ba'i don yod 
8a4(rgyud): stobs//de bzhin gshegs pa'i yid ltar mgyog//rgyal ba dgra rgyal rnam par rgyal//'khor lo bsgyur ba stobs po che//tshogs kyi slob dpon tshogs kyi mchog/
8a5(rgyud): tshogs rje tshogs bdag dbang dang ldan//mthu chen bces par 'dzin pa ste//tshul chen gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog//tshig rje tshig bdag smras mkhas pa//tshig la dbang ba tshig
8a6(rgyud): mtha' yas//tshig bden pa dang bden par smra//bden pa bzhi ni ston pa po//phyir mi ldog pa phyir mi 'ong //'dren pa rang rgyal bse ru'i tshul//nges 'byung sna 
8a7(rgyud): tshogs las 'byung ba//'byung ba chen po rgyu gcig po//dge slong dgra bcom zag pa zad//'dod chags bral bas dbang po thul//bde ba rnyed pas 'jigs med thob|
8b1(rgyud): bsil bar gyur pas rnyog pa med//rig pa dang ni rkang par ldan//bde gshegs 'jig rten rig pa'i mchog/bdag gir mi 'dzin ngar mi 'dzin//bden pa gnyis kyi 
8b2(rgyud): tshul la gnas//'khor ba'i pha rol mthar son pa//bya ba byas pa skam sar gnas//shes pa 'ba' zhig nges bsal ba//shes rab mtshon chas rnams 'joms pa/
8b3(rgyud): dam chos chos rgyal gsal bar ldan//'jig rten snang bar byed pa'i mchog/chos kyi dbang phyug chos kyi rgyal//legs pa'i lam ni ston pa po//don grub bsam pa 
8b4(rgyud): grub pa ste//kun tu rtog pa thams cad spangs//rnam par mi rtog dbyings mi zad//chos dbyings dam pa zad mi shes//bsod nams ldan pa bsod nams tshogs/
8b5(rgyud): ye shes ye shes 'byung gnas che//ye shes ldan pa yod med shes//tshogs gnyis tshogs ni bsags pa po//rtag pa kun rgyal rnal 'byor can//bsam gtan bsam 
8b6(rgyud): bya blo ldan mchog/so sor rang rig mi g.yo ba//mchog dbang po sku gsum 'chang //sangs rgyas lnga'i bdag nyid can//khyab bdag ye shes lnga'i bdag/sangs 
8b7(rgyud): rgyas lnga bdag cod pan can//spyan lnga chags pa med pa 'chang //sangs rgyas thams cad bskyed pa po//sangs rgyas sras po dam pa'i mchog/shes pa srid 'byung skye gnas med//chos las 'byung ba 
9a1(rgyud): srid pa sel//gcig pu sra 'khregs rdo rje'i bdag/skyes ma thag tu 'gro ba'i bdag/nam mkha' las 'byung rang 'byung ba//shes 
9a2(rgyud): rab ye shes me bo che//'od chen rnam par snang bar byed//ye shes snang ba las me ba//'gro ba'i mar me ye shes sgron//gzi brjid phung po 'od bsal ba//sngags mchog 
9a3(rgyud): mnga' bdag rigs sngags rgyal//gsang sngags rgyal po don chen byed//gtsug tor chen po rmad 'byung gtsug/nam mkha'i bdag po sna tshogs ston//sangs rgyas kun 
9a4(rgyud): bdag dngos po mchog/'gro kun dga' ba'i mig dang ldan//sna tshogs gzugs can bskyed pa po//mchod cing brjod pa drang srong che//rigs gsum 'chang ba gsang 
9a5(rgyud): sngags 'chang //dam tshig chen po gsang sngags 'dzin//gtso bo dkon mchog gsum 'dzin pa//theg pa mchog gsum ston pa po//don yod zhags pa rnam par rgyal//
9a6(rgyud): 'dzin pa chen po rdo rje zhabs//rdo rje lcags kyu zhags pa che//chos kyi dbyings kyi ye shes la bstod pa'i//rdo rje 'jigs byed 'jigs par byed//
9a7(rgyud): khro bo'i rgyal po gdong drug 'jigs//mig drug lag drug stobs dang ldan//keng rus mche ba brtsigs pa po//ha la ha la gdong brgya pa//gshin rje gshed po 
9b1(rgyud): bgegs kyi rgyal//rdo rje shugs chen 'jigs par byed//rdo rje grags pa rdo rje snying //sgyu 'phrul rdo rje gsus po che//rdo rje las skyes rdo rje'i bdag/rdo rje snying po mkha' 
9b2(rgyud): 'dra ba//mi g.yo ral pa gcig gis bgyings//glang chen ko rlon gos su gyon//grag chen ha ha zhes sgrogs pa//hi hi zhes sgrogs 'jigs par byed//gad mo chen po 
9b3(rgyud): gad rgyangs can//rdo rje gad mo cher sgrogs pa//rdo rje sems dpa' sems dpa' che//rdo rje rgyal po bde ba che//rdo rje drag po dga' ba che//rdo rje hU~M ste hU~M zhes sgrogs//
9b4(rgyud): mtshon du rdo rje mda' thogs pa//rdo rje ral gris ma lus gcod//rdo rje kun 'chang rdo rje can//rdo rje gcig pu g.yul sel ba//rdo rje 'bar ba mig mi zad//skra 
9b5(rgyud): yang rdo rje 'bar ba ste//rdo rje 'bebs pa 'bebs pa che//mig brgya pa ste rdo rje'i mig/lus ni rdo rje ba spu can//rdo rje'i spu ni gcig spu'i lus//sen mo skyes pa 
9b6(rgyud): rdo rje'i rtse//rdo rje snying po lpags pa mkhregs//rdo rje'i phreng thogs dpal dang ldan//rdo rje'i rgyan gyis brgyan pa ste//gang rgyangs ha ha nges par sgrogs//ye ge drug 
9b7(rgyud): pa rdo rje'i sgra//'jam dbyangs chen po dgra che ba//'jig rten gsum na sgra gcig pa//nam mkha'i khams las sgra sgrogs pa//sgra dang ldan pa rnams kyi mchog/

10a3(rgyud): phyogs bcu chos kyi rnga bo che//gzugs med gzugs bzang dam pa ste//sna tshogs gzugs can yid las skyes//gzugs rnams thams cad snang ba'i dpal//

10a7(rgyud): med|/namakha'i mtha' la longs spyod pa//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes mtsho//ma rig sgo nga'i sbubs 'byed pa//srid pa'i dra ba 'joms pa po//nyon mongs 
10b1(rgyud): ma lus zhi byed pa//'khor ba'i rgya mtsho pha rol phyin//ye shes dbang skur cod pan can//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rgyan du thogs//sdug bsngal gsum gyi sdug 
10b2(rgyud): bsngal zhi//gsum sel mtha' yas grol gsum thob//sgrib pa kun las nges par grol//mkha' ltar mnyam pa nyid la gnas//nyon mongs dri ma kun las 'das//
10b3(rgyud): dus gsum dus med rtogs pa po//sems can kun gyi glu chen po//yon tan thod can rnams kyi thod//nyon mongs kun las rnam par grol//nam mkha'i lam la rab 
10b4(rgyud): gnas pa//yid bzhin nor bu chen po 'chang //khyab bdag rin chen kun gyi mchog/dpag bsam shing chen rgyas pa ste//bum pa bzang po che ba'i mchog/byed pa 
10b5(rgyud): sems can kun don byed//phan 'dogs sems can gnyis gshin pa//bzang ngan shes shing dus shes pa//khyab bdag dam shes dam tshig ldan//dus shes sems 
10b6(rgyud): can dbang po shes//rnam grol gsum la mkhas pa po//yon tan ldan zhing yon tan shes//chos shes bkra shis bkra shis 'byung //bkra shis kun gyi bkra shis 
10b7(rgyud): pa//grags pa'i bkra shis snyan grags dge//dbugs 'byin chen po dga' ston che//dga' chen rol mo chen po ste//bkur sti rim gro phun sum tshogs//
11a1(rgyud): @#/ O /mchog du dga' ba'i grags dag dpal//mchog ldan mchog sbyin gtso bo ste//skyabs kyi dam pa skyabs su 'os//'jigs chen dgra ste 
11a2(rgyud): rab kyi mchog//'jigs pa ma lus sel ba po//tsug pud pud bu lcang lo can//ral pa mun dza cod pan thogs//gdong lnga gtsug pud lnga dang ldan//zur phud lnga pa 
11a3(rgyud): me tog thod//mgo zlum brtul shugs chen po pa//tshangs par spyod pa rtul zhugs mchog//dka' thub mthar phyin dka' thub che//gtsang gnas dam pa go'u tam/
11a4(rgyud): bram ze tshangs pa tshangs pa che//mya ngan 'das pa tshangs pas thob//grol ba thar pa rnam grol lus//rnam grol zhi ba zhi ba nyid//mya ngan 'das zhi mya ngan 'das//
11a5(rgyud): legs par mya ngan 'das dang nye//bde sdug sel ba mthar gyur pa//chags bral lus las 'das pa po//thub pa med pa dpe med pa//mi mngon mi snang bsal byed min|
11a6(rgyud): mi 'gyur kun 'gro khyab pa po//phra zhing zag med sa bon bral//rdul med rdul bral dri ma med//nyes pa spangs pa skyon med pa//shin du sad pa sad pa'i bdag//kun
11a7(rgyud): shes kun rigs dam pa po//rnam par shes pa'i chos nyid 'das//ye shes gnyis med tshul 'chang ba//rnam par rtog med lhun gyi grub//dus gsum sangs rgyas las byed pa/
11b1(rgyud): sangs rgyas thog ma tha ma med//dang po'i sangs rgyas rgyu med pa//ye shes mig gcig dri med//ye shes lus can de bzhin gshegs//rig gi dbang phyug smra ba che//smra ba'i skyes mchog smra ba'i   
11b2(rgyud): rgyal||smra ba'i dam pa mchog gi gnas//smra ba'i seng ge tshugs pa med//kun tu lta ba Igtu dga'//gzi brjid phreng ba blta na sdug//'od bzang 'bar 
11b3(rgyud): ba dpal gyi be'u //lag na 'od 'bar snang ba po//sman pa che mchog gtso bo ste//zung rngu 'byin pa bla na med//sman rnams ma lus ljon pa'i shing //nad do cog 
11b4(rgyud): gi dgra che ba//sdug gu 'jig rten gsum gyi mchog//dpal ldan rgyu skar dkyil 'khor can//phyogs bcu nam mkha'i mthar thug par//chos kyi rgyal mtshan 
11b5(rgyud): legs par 'dzugs//'gro na gdugs gcig yangs pa ste//byams dang snying rje'i dkyil 'khor can//dpal ldan pad ma gar gyi bdag//khyab bdag 
11b6(rgyud): chen po rin chen gdugs//sangs rgyas kun gyi gzi brjid che//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku 'chang ba//sangs rgyas kun gyi rnal 'byor che//sangs rgyas kun gyi bstan pa  
11b7(rgyud): gcig//rdo rje rin chen dbang skur dpal//rin chen kun bdag dbang phyug ste//'jig rten dbang phyug kun gyi bdag//rdo rje 'chang ba kun gyi rje//sangs rgyas 
12a1(rgyud): kun gyi thugs che ba//sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs la gnas//sangs rgyas kun gyi sku che ba//sangs rgyas kun kyi gsung yang yin//rdo 
12a2(rgyud): rje nyi ma snang ba che//rdo rje zla ba dri med 'od//chags bral la sogs chags pa che//kha dog sna tshogs 'bar ba'i 'od//rdo rje dkyil dkrung rdzogs sangs rgyas//sangs rgyas 'gro ba'i 
12a3(rgyud): chos 'dzin pa//dpal ldan sangs rgyas pad ma skyes//kun mkhyen ye shes mdzod 'dzin pa//rgyal po sgyu 'phrul sna tshogs 'chang //che ba sangs rgyas rigs sngags rgyal//
12a4(rgyud): rdo rje rnon po ral gri che//yi ge mchog ste rnam par dag/theg pa chen pos sdug bsngal gcod//mtshon chen po rdo rje chos//rdo rje zab mo 'dzin na 'jig/rdo rje blo gros don 
12a5(rgyud): bzhin rigs//pha rol phyin pa kun rdzogs pa//sa rnams kun gyi rgyan dang ldan//rnam par dag pa bdag med chos//yang dag ye shes zla 'od bzang //brtson chen sgyu 
12a6(rgyud): 'phrul dra ba la//rgyud kun gyi ni bdag po mchog/rdo rje gdan ni ma lus ldan//ye shes sku rnams ma lus 'chang //kun tu bzang po blo gros bzang //sa yi snying po 'gro 
12a7(rgyud): ba 'dzin//sangs rgyas kun gyi snying po che//sprul pa'i 'khor lo sna tshogs 'chang //dngos po kun gyi rang bzhin mchog/dngos po kun gyi rang ba zhin 'dzin//so sor 
12b1(rgyud): rtogs pa'i ye shes la stod pa'i IIIII //skye med chos te sna tshogs don//chos kun ngo bo nyid 'chang ba//shes rab chen po skad     
12b2(rgyud): cig la//chos kun khong du chud pa 'chang //chos kun mngon par rtogs pa ste//thub pa blo mchog 'byung po'i mtha'//mi g.yo rab tu dang ba'i bdag//rdzogs pa'i sangs 
12b3(rgyud): rgyas byang chub 'chang //sangs rgyas kun gyi mngon gsum pa//ye shes me lce 'od rab gsal//'dod pa'i don grub dam pa ste//ngan song thams cad rnam sbyong ba//
12b4(rgyud): mgon po sems can kun gyi mchog//sems can thams cad rab grol byed//nyon mongs g.yul du gcig dpa' ba//mi shes dgra yi dregs pa 'joms//blo ldan sgeg
12b5(rgyud): 'chang dpal dang ldan//brtan po mi sdug gzugs 'chang ba//lag pa brgya po kun skyod cing //gom pa'i stabs kyi gar byed pa//dpal ldan lag pa brgyas gang la/
12b6(rgyud): nam mkha' khyab par gar byed pa//sa yi dkyil 'khor bzhi'i khyon//rkang pa ya gcig mthil gyis gnon//rkang 'theb ser mo'i khyon gyis kyang //tshangs pa'i yul 
12b7(rgyud): sa rtse nas gnon//don gcig gnyis med chos kyi don//dam pa'i don te 'jigs pa med//rnam rig sna tshogs gzugs don can//sems dang rnam shes rgyud dang ldan|
13a1(rgyud): @#/ O /dngos don ma lus rnams la dga'//stong pa nyid dga' 'dod chags blo//srid pa'i 'dod chags sogs spangs pa//srid gsum 
13a2(rgyud): dga' ba chen po pa//sprin dkar dag pa bzhin du dkar //'od bzangs ston ka'i zla ba'i 'od//nyi ma'i char ka'i dkyil ltar mdzes//sen mo'i 'od ni shas cher dmar|
13a3(rgyud): cod pan bzang po mthon ka'i rtse//skra mchog 'thon ka chen po can//nor bu chen po 'od chags dpal//sangs rgyas sprul pa'i rgyan dang ldan//'jig rten khams brgya 
13a4(rgyud): kun skyod cing //rdzu 'phrul rkang pa'i stobs dang ldan//de nyid dran pa chen po 'chang//dran pa bzhi po'i ting 'dzin rgyal//byang chub yan lag me tog spos//de bzhin
13a5(rgyud): gshegs pa'i yon tan mtsho//lam gyi yan lag brgyad tshul rigs//yang dag sangs rgyas lam rig pa//sems can kun la shas cher chags//nam mkha' 
13a6(rgyud): lta bur chags pa med//sems can gyi yid la 'jug//sems can kun gyi yid ltar 'gyog+sa//sems can kun gyi dbang don shes//sems can kun gyis yid 'phrog
13a7(rgyud): pa||pung po lnga don de nyid shes//rnam dag pung po lnga 'chang ba//nges 'byung kun gyi mtha' la gnas//nges par 'byung ba kun la mkhas//nges 'byung kun gyi 
13b1(rgyud): la gnas//nges par 'byung ba kun ston pa//yan lag bcu gnyis srid rtsa bton//dag pa rnam pa bcu gnyis 'chang //bden bzhi'i tshul gyi rnam pa can//shes pa 
13b2(rgyud): brgyad po rtogs pa 'chang //bden don rnam pa bcu gnyis ldan//de nyid rnam pa bcu drug rig/rnam pa nyi shus byang chub pa//rnam par sangs rgyas kun rig mchog/
13b3(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi sprul pa'i sku//bye ba dpag med 'gyed pa po//skad cig thams cad mngon par rtogs//sems kyi skad cig don kun rig/theg pa sna tshogs thabs 
13b4(rgyud): tshul gyis//'gro ba'i don la rtogs pa po//theg pa gsum gyi nges 'byung la//theg pa gcig gi 'bras bur gnas//nyon mongs khams rnams dag pa'i bdag/las 
13b5(rgyud): kyi khams rnams zad byed pa//chu bo'i rgya mtsho kun las rgal//sbyor ba'i dgon pa las byung ba//nyon mongs nye ba kun nyon mongs//bag chags bcas pa gtan spangs 
13b6(rgyud): pa//snying rje chen po shes rab thabs//don yod 'gro ba'i don byed pa//'du shes kun gyi don spangs shing //rnams shes don ni 'gag par byed//sems can 
13b7(rgyud): kun yid yul dang ldan//sems can kun gyi yid rig pa//sems can kun gyi yis la gnas//de dag sems dang 'thun par 'jug/sems can kun yid tshim par byed//
14a1(rgyud): @#/ O /sems can kun gyi yid dga' ba//grub pa mthar phyin 'khrul pa med//nor pa thams cad rnam par spangs//don gsum tha tsom med pa'i 
14a2(rgyud): blo||kun don yon tan gsum gyi bdag//phung po lnga don dus gsum du//skad cig thams cad bye brag phyed//skad cig gcig gis rdzogs sangs rgyas//sangs rgyas 
14a3(rgyud): kun gyi rang bzhin 'chang //lus med lus ste lus kyi mchog//lus kyi mtha' ni rtogs pa po//gzugs rnams sna tshogs kun du ston//nor bu chen po rin chen tog/
14a4(rgyud): mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes kyi I ////kun gyi rtogs bya ba//sangs rgyas byang chub bla na med//gsang sngags las byung yi ge 
14a5(rgyud): med//gsang sngags chen po rigs gsum pa//gsang sngagadon kun skyed pa po//thig le chen po yi ge med//stong pa chen po yi ge med //thig le stong pa yi ge 
14a6(rgyud): brgya||rnam pa kun ldan rnam pa med//bcu drug phyed phyed thig le can//yan lag med pa'i rtsis las 'das//bsam gtan bzhi pa'i rtse mo can//bsam   
14a7(rgyud): gtan yan lag kun shes shing //ting 'dzin rig dang rgyud rig pa//ting 'dzin lus can lus kyi mchog//longs spyod rdzogs sku kun las rgyal//sprul pa'i (sku)ste sku 
14b1(rgyud): sku'i mchog//sangs rgyas sprul pa'i rgyud 'chang ba//phyogs bcur sprul pa sna tshogs 'gyed//ji bzhin 'gro ba'i don byed pa//lha dang lha dbang lha yi lha//lha'i 
14b2(rgyud): bdag po lha min bdag//'chi med dbang po lha'i bla//'joms byed 'joms pa dbang phyug pe//srid pa'i dgon pa las rgal ba//ston pa gcig pu 'gro ba'i bla//'jig 
14b3(rgyud): rten phyogs bcur rab grags pa//chos kyi sbyin bdag che ba po//byams pa'i go cha chas pa ste//snying rje yi ni yan lag bgos//shes rab ral gri mda' gzhu thog+sa/
14b4(rgyud): nyon mongs mi shes g.yul ngo sel//dpa' bo bdud dgra bdud 'dul//bdud bzhi'i 'jigs pa sel bar byed//bdud kyi dpungs rnams pham byed pa//rdzogs pa'i 
14b5(rgyud): sangs rgyas 'jig rten 'dren//mchod 'os stod 'os phyag gi gnas//rtag tu rim gro bya ba'i 'os//bkur 'os brjed par bya ba'i mchog//phyag byar 'os pa bla ma'i 
14b6(rgyud): rab//'jig rten gsum po gom gcig bgrod//mkha' ltar mtha' med rnam par gnon//gsum rig gtsang ma dag pa ste//mngon shes drug ldan rjes dran drug/
14b7(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che//rdzu 'phrul chen po'i 'jig rten 'das//shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i mtha'//shes rab kyis ni de nyid thob//bdag rigs gzhan 
15a1(rgyud): @#/ O /rig thams cad la//kun la phan pa'i gang zag mchog/dper bya kun las 'das pa ste//shes dang shes bya'i bdag po mchog/gtso bo
15a2(rgyud): chos kyi sbyin ste//phyag rgya bzhi po'i don ston pa//'gro ba'i bsnyen bkur gnas kyi mchog//nges 'byung gsum po bgrod rnams kyi//don gyi dam pa rnam dag dpal//
15a3(rgyud): 'jig rten gsum na bskal bzang che//dpal ldan 'byor pa kun byed pa//'jam dpal dpal dang ldan pa'i mchog//bya ba grub pa'i ye shes la bstod pa'i  ེུ I
15a4(rgyud): ུ ////rdo rje mchog khyod 'dud //yang dag mthar gyur khyod la 'dud//stod nyid las byung khyod la 'dud//sangs rgyas byang chub   
15a5(rgyud): khyod la 'dud //sangs rgyas chags pa khyod la 'dud//sangs rgyas 'dod la phyag 'tshal 'dud//sangs rgyas dges pa khyod la 'dud//sangs rgyas rol la phyag 
15a6(rgyud): 'tshal 'dud//sangs rgyas 'dzum pa khyod la 'dud//sang rgyas bzhad la phyag 'tshal 'dud/sangs rgyas gsung nyid khyod la 'dud//sangs rgyas thugs
15a7(rgyud): la phyag 'tshal 'dud//med pa las byung khyod la 'dud//sangs rgyas 'byung ba khyod la 'dud//nam mkha' las byung khyod la 'dud //ye shes (las)byung khyod 
15b1(rgyud): la 'dud//sgyu 'phrul dra ba khyod la 'dud//sangs rgyas rol ston khyod la 'dud//thams cad thams cad khyod la 'dud//ye shes sku nyid khyod la 'dud//ye shas 
15b2(rgyud): lnga'i sgo nas rigs lnga la bstod pa'i ye shes bcu+is pa'o//7//lag na rdo rje rdo rje 'chang //'di ni bcom ldan 'das kyi ye shes sku//de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
15b3(rgyud): cad kyi ye shes kyi sku//'jam dpal ye shes sems dpa'i yongs su dag pa thun mong ma yin pa'i ming yang dag pa brjod pa yin te//khyod kyi dga' ba dang /dad pa dang /
15b4(rgyud): mgu ba chen po bla na med pa bskyed pa dang //lus dang ngag dang yid kyi gsang ba yongs su dag par bya ba dang /yongs su ma rdzogs pa dang //yongs su dag pa'i pha rol tu phyin 
15b5(rgyud): pa dang //bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs rnams yongs su rdzogs shing //yongs su dag par bya ba dag//bla na med pa'i don ma rtogs pa rtogs par bya ba dang /
15b6(rgyud): ma thob pa thob par bya ba nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thabs cad kyi dam pa'i chos kyi tshul yang dag par bzung ba'i phyir//ngas bshad cing bstan te dgrol zhing rnam 
15b7(rgyud): par phye la gsal bar byas so//byin gyis brlab par bya'o//lag na rdo rje rdo rje 'chang //'di ni ngas khyod kyi rgyud la yang gsang sngags thams cad kyi chos nyid 
16a1(rgyud): @#//byin gyis brlabs kyis byin gyis brlabs so//lag na rdo rje 'chang //gzhan yang ming yang dag par brjod pa 'di ni de 
16a2(rgyud): bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi shin tu yongs su dag cing yongs su byang ba'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes dang //sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi gsang bar gyur pa'o//
16a3(rgyud): shin tu yongs su dag cing shin tu yongs su byang ba'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thams can kyi sangs rgyas kyi byang chub bo//yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 
16a4(rgyud): rnams kyi mngon par rtogs pa'o//bde bar gshegs pa rnams kyi//de bzhin gshegs pa thabs cad kyi chos kyi dbyings bla na med pa thugs su chud pa'o//
16a5(rgyud): rgyal ba rnams kyi bdud kyi stobs thams cad pham par mdzad pa'o//stobs bcu dang ldan pa rnams kyi stobs bcu'i stobs nyid do//thams cad mkhyen pa rnams 
16a6(rgyud): kyi thams cad mkhyen pa nyid do//sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad kyi lung ngo //sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yang dag par bsgrubs pa'o//byang chub sems 
16a7(rgyud): dpa' sems dpa' chen po thams cad kyi bsod nams dang //ye shes kyi tshogs yongs su rdzogs pa dri ma med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//nyan thos dang rang 
16b1(rgyud): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi skye gnas so//lha dang mi thams cad phun sum tshogs pa thams cad kyi zhing ngo //theg pa chen po'i gzhi'o//byang chub sems dpa'i 
16b2(rgyud): spyod pa rnams kyi 'byung khungs so//'phags pa'i lam thams cad kyi mtha'o//rnam par grol ba rnams brtags pa'o//nges par 'byung ba'i lam skye ba'i 
16b3(rgyud): gnas so//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gdung mi 'chad pa'o//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i rigs dang rgyud rab tu 'phel ba'o//pha rol rgol 
16b4(rgyud): ba thams cad tshar gcod pa'o//mu stegs can thams cad rnam par 'joms pa'o//bdud bzhi'i stobs kyi dpung gi sde pam par byed pa'o//sems can 
16b5(rgyud): thams cad yang dag par bsdud pa'o//nges par 'byung ba bgrod pa thams cad kyi 'phags pa'i lam yongs su smin pa'o//tshangs pa'i bzhi la gnas pa rnams 
16b6(rgyud): kyi ting nge 'dzin to//sems rtse gcig pa rnams kyi bsam gtan no//lus dang ngag dang yid 'dul ba la brtson pa rnams kyi rnal 'byor ro//kun tu sbyor ba 
16b7(rgyud): thams cad dang bral ba'o//nyon mongs pa dang nye ba'i nyon mongs pa thams cad spong ba'o//sgrib pa thams cad nye bar zhi ba'o//'ching ba thams cad las rnam 
17a1(rgyud): @#/ O /par grol ba'o//rdzas thams cad las thar ba'o//sems kyi g.yeng ba thams cad zhi ba'o//'byor pa thaMd kyi 'byung gnas so//rgud pa thams cad kyi 'byung 
17a2(rgyud): gnas so//rgud pa thaMd sel ba'o//ngan song gi sgo thams (ca) gcod pa'o//rnam par grol ba'i grong khyer gyi lam bzang po'o//'khor ba'i 'khor lo la mi 'jug pa'o//chos kyi 'khor lo 
17a3(rgyud): bskor ba'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stan pa'i gdugs dang //rgyal mtshan dang ba dan bsgreng ba'o//dam pa'i chos ston pa'i gnas so//byang chub sems dpa' gsang sngags kyi sgo'i 
17a4(rgyud): spyad pa spyod pa rnams kyi myur du grub pa'o//byang chub sems dpa' shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la mngon par brtson pa rnams kyi sgom pa rtogs pa'o//gnyis su med pa'i so sor rtog pa 
17a5(rgyud): bsgom pa la mngon par brtson par rnams kyi stong pa nyid so sor rtog+sa pa'o//gnyis su med par so sor rtogs par mngon par brtson pa rnamasakyi rdzogs pa'o//pha rol du phyin pa dang tshogs thaMd 
17a6(rgyud): nges par 'grub pa'o//sa thaMd yongs su rdzogs pa'o//'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi po rnams yang dag par 'grub pa'o//chos thams cad kyi dran pa nye bar bzhag pa bzhi po rnams kyi 
17a7(rgyud): sems rtse gcig so sor rtogs pa'o//mtshan yang dag par brjod pa 'di ni sangs rgyas kyi yon tan thams cad kyi bar du yang yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'o//lag na rdo rje rdo rje 'chang /
17b1(rgyud): gzhan yang ming yang dag par brjod pa 'di ni sems can thaMd kyi lus dang ngag dang yid las kun du spyod pa'i sdig pa ma lus pa rab du zhi bar byed pa'o//sems can thams cad kyi ngan song thaMd 
17b2(rgyud): rnam par sbyong ba'o//ngan 'gro thams cad bzlog pa'o//las kyi sgrib pa thams cad yang dag par bcod pa'o//mi khom pa thams cad kyi skye ba'i rgyun mi skye bar byed pa'o//'jig+sa pa chen 
17b3(rgyud): po brgyad zhi bar byed pa'o//rmi lam ngan pa thaMd nges par 'jig par byed pa'o//ltas ngan pa thams cad nges par sel ba'o//brda' ngas dang dgra bgegs thams cad rnam par zhi bar byed pa'o//
17b4(rgyud): bdud kyi dgra'i las thaMd ring du spong bar byed pa'o//dge ba'i rtsa ba dang bsod nams thams cad 'phel bar byed pa'o//tshul bzhin ma yin pa'i yid la byed pa thaMd mi skye bar byed pa'o//
17b5(rgyud): rgyag+sa pa dang nga rgyal dang dreg+sa pa dang ngar 'dzin pa ma lus par 'joms par byed pa'o//sdug bsngal ba dang //yid mi bde ba thams cad mi skye bar byed pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi snying 
17b6(rgyud): por 'gyur ba'o//byang chub sems dpa' thaMd kyi gsang bar 'gyur pa'o//nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsang chen du gyur pa'o//phyag rgya dang gsang sngags thaMd du gyur pa'o//chos 
17b7(rgyud): thaMd brjod du med par smra ba rnaMs kyi dran pa dang shes bzhin yang dag par bskyed pa'o//bla na med pa'i shes rab bskyed pa dang //yid gzhungs par byed pa'o//nad med pa dang //
18a1(rgyud): stobs dang dbang phyug phun sum tshogs par byed pa'o//dpal dang dge ba dang zhi ba dang legs pa rab tu 'phel bar byed pa'o//
18a2(rgyud): grags pa dang snyen pa dang //tshigs su bcad pa dang //bstod pa dang grags par byed pa'o//nad dang 'jigs pa chen po thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa'o//
18a3(rgyud): shin tu dag pa rnams kyi yang shin tu dag pa'o//shin tu dag byed rnams kyi yang shin du dag byed do//rab tu byang ba rnams kyi yang rab tu byang ba'o//rab tu bkra shis pa 
18a4(rgyud): rnams kyi yang rab tu bkra shis pa'o//skyabs 'dod pa rnaMs kyi yang skyabs so//gnas med pa rnams kyi yang gnas so//skyob pa 'dod pa rnams kyi yang skyob pa'o//
18a5(rgyud): dpung snyan 'dod pa rnams kyi yang dpung gnyen no//gling 'dod pa rnams kyi gling ngo //'khra sa 'dod pa rnams kyi khra sa bla na med pa'o//srid pa'i rgya mtsho'i pha rol du 
18a6(rgyud): 'gro ba rnams kyi gru'o//nad thams cad sel bar byed pa'i sman gyi rgyal po'o//blang bya dang dor bya'i dngos po bye brag phyed pa'i shes rab nyid do//lta ba ngan pa'i 
18a7(rgyud): mun nag thams cad yang dag par sel bar byed pa'i ye shes kyi snang ba'o//sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du yongs su rdzogs 
18b1(rgyud): par byed pa'i yid bzhin gyi nor bu lta bu'o//'jam dpal ye shes kyi sku thob par bya ba'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes so//spyan lnga thob par bya ba'i 
18b2(rgyud): ye shes mthong ba yongs su thob pa'o//zang zing dang mi 'jigs pa dang chos kyi sbyin pa gtong ba'i phyir pha rol tu phyin pa drug yongs su rdzogs pa'o//bsod nams 
18b3(rgyud): dang ye shes kyi tshogs dang ting nge 'dzin yongs su rdzogs pa'i phyir sa bcu yongs su rdzogs pa'o//chos nyid dang bral ba'i phyir gnyis su med pa'i chos nyid do//sgro btags 
18b4(rgyud): pa med pa'i phyir de bzhin nyid dang gzhan ma yin pa'i chos nyid do//de bzhin gshegs pa'i yongs su dag pa'i ngo bo nyid yin pa'i mtha'i rang bzhin nyid do//lta ba ngan pa'i 
18b5(rgyud): tshang tshing brgya stong ma lus pa sel ba'i phyir rnam pa thams cad rnam pa med pa stong pa nyid kyi rang bzhin nyid do//mtshan yang dag par brjod pa 'di ni 'di lta ste//gnyis su 
18b6(rgyud): med pa'i chos nyid kyi don kyi mtshan yang dag par 'dzin pa ston pa'i phyir chos thams cad brjod du med pa'i rang bzhin nyid do//lag na rdo rje rdo rje 'chang //gzhan yang rigs
18b7(rgyud): kyi bu 'am rigs kyi bu mo gsang sngags kyi sgo'i spyad pa spyod pa gang la la/bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal ye shes sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thams
19a1(rgyud): @#/ O /cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes sku//gnyis su med pa'i don dam pa'i mtshan yang dag par brjod pa mtshan gyi gtsug gi nor bu 'di thams   
19a2(rgyud): cad yongs su rdzogs pa dang //ma nyams shing lhag chad med par tshigs su bcad pa'i tshig dang //yig 'bru 'di dag nyid kyis nyin gcig bzhin du dus gsum du 'chang ba 
19a3(rgyud): dang ||klog pa dang kun chub par byed pa dang //tshul bzhin du yid la byed pa dang //gzhan dag la yang dam tshigs bzhin du ji lta ba bzhin du ston pa dang /ming gi don 
19a4(rgyud): gang yang rung ba re re nas kyang 'jam dpal ye shes sems pa'i sku dmigs te kun nas sgo'i gnas pas gnas pa dang //chos thams cad rtogs pa'i mchog rnyog pa med pa'i 
19a5(rgyud): dad pa dang ldan bzhin pa dang //mos pa dang /de kho na nyid yid la byed pas/sems rtse gcig tu sgom pa de la ni/dus gsum gyi dus med pa dang ldan pa'i sangs rgyas 
19a6(rgyud): dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad 'dus te gshegs nas/chos kyi sgo thams cad ston par mdzad de/sku yang ston to//sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa'  tham+sa
19a7(rgyud): cad kyis byin gyis rlob par mdzad de/sku dang gsung dang thugs thams cad kyis de'i rgyud la yang dag par byin gyis slob po//sangs rgyas dang byang chu ba sems dpa' thaMd 
19b1(rgyud): kyis phan gdags pas phan 'dogs par mdzad do//chos thams cad la mi 'jigs pa yang grub par mdzad do//dgra bcom pa dang nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad 
19b2(rgyud): dang 'phag pa'i chos la sdug par 'dzin pa'i bsams pas bdag nyid kyi dngos po yang nye bar ston par mdzad do//ma rungs pa 'dul ba'i khro bo'i rgyal po rnams dang /rdo 
19b3(rgyud): rje 'chang chen po la sogs pa 'gro ba yongs su skyob pa rnams sprul pa'i sku sna tshogs kyi mdangs dang /stobs dang gzi brjid zil gyis mi non pa dang /gsang sngags dang /
19b4(rgyud): phyag rgya dang /mngon par rtogs pa dang /dkyil 'khor thams cad sgrub par byed pa'o//gsang sngags dang rig sngags kyi rgyal mo ma lus pa dang //bgegs dang /log
19b5(rgyud): 'dren dang /bdud kyi dgra dang phyir bzlog pa dang /gzhan gyis mi thub pa thams cad kyang nyin mtshan phyad par skad cig re re la spyod lam thams du bsrung ba dang /skyob pa dang /
19b6(rgyud): sbed par byed do//tshangs pa dang /dbang po dang nye dbang po dang /drag po dang mtshan po che dang //kun 'gyed gzhon nu dang //dbang phyug chen po dang /smin drug gi bu dang /
19b7(rgyud): nag po chen po dang /dga' byed dbang phyug dang /gshin rje dang /chu lha dang /ku be ra dang /'phrog ma dang /phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten skyong ba gang yin pa de dag kyang /rtag

6a3(rgyud2): zla ba mtha' dag dang ni dus dang dus gzhi dang ni me dus bgrod pa rnam pa gnyis//rgyal po lo ni mtha' dag rjes su nyin zhag dus sbyor chu tshod dag dang chu yi srang rnams te//'di 
6a4(rgyud2): rnams de ltar bgrod pa lus skyes phra dang mi dang 'byung po lha mtshan rnams dang ni//nus pa dang gtso bo'i zhag re la ste srid pa gsum gyi gnas dang lus kyi dbus su de bzhin no//
6a5(rgyud2): lo 'di nas ni drug brgya'i lo yis gsal bar mi bdag grags pa sham b+ha la zhes bya bar 'byung //de nas klu'i lo brgya rnams kyis nges par mkha'i yul du kla klo'i chos dag 
6a6(rgyud2): rab du 'jug/de yi dus su 'dzin ma la ni nyung du'i byed pa gsal bar mi rnams dag gis rig par bya//mtha' dgas bzhi la ni grub pa'i mtha' rnams rnam par nyams pa 
6a7(rgyud2): dus kyis sbyor ba la 'byung 'gyur//me mkha' rgya mtsho rnams la rab byung la sogs 'das pa rnams bsres kla klo'i lo ni rab tu grub//kla klo dbang po'i lo ni lag pa gdengs 
6b1(rgyud2): can zla bas dman pa'i lhag ma dag kyang nyi mas bsgyur //nag pa la sogs zla ba bsres te 'og tu dus kyis bsgyur ba mkha' me zla bas rnam par dbye//rnyed pa steng du bsres 
6b2(rgyud2): pa dag ni mi yi bdag po zla ba'i tshogs pa rnams par dag par 'gyur//zla ba gnas gsum gyur pa'i dbus ma'i phung po dag la gang gnas lag pa rtse mo can gyis bsgyur /
6b3(rgyud2): rtsa ba dag la drug gi chas rnyed de yang bu lon du 'gyur dbus ma la ni sbyang bar bya//steng 'og dag la mig dang sum bcu'i nor ni bsres pa gsal bar bya ste drug bcu'i cha dag 
6b4(rgyud2): gis//rnyed pa dag tu rab bsres thub pa mchog rnams dag gis bcad pa'i lhag ma dag ni bza' ru 'gyur //gnas gnyis nyi zla bsres pa dag las dus dang nyi mas thob 
6b5(rgyud2): la 'byung ba bsres pa mtha' dag ni//de yi steng gi phung por sbyin te dang po lag pas bsgyur la'o brgyad dang gnyis kyis phye ba chogs//gnas gsum dbu ma dag ni dbang 
6b6(rgyud2): phyug gis bsgyur 'og ma dgu dang yon tan rnams kyi tsha thob par//gnyis bsres dman pa'i dbu mar chu tshod dag gis thob pa nor te steng gi phung por gnyis kyis ni//bsgyur 
6b7(rgyud2): nas byin la skar mas bgos lhag mi bdag nyi ma'i skar ma la sogs longs spyod dag tu 'gyur //gza' la gza' ni rab sbyin mi bdag nyis 'gyur      chu tshod rnams te 
7a1(rgyud2): @#/ O /'og tu tshogs dag la yang gnyis//skar ma gnyis dang chu tshod drag po nor bu rab gsal nyi ma'i longs spyod dag la rab du sbyin//'di dag 
7a2(rgyud2): zla ba'i nges par 'gyur te slar yang zla ba zla ba dag la rab tu sbyin par gsungs//tshes rnams sbyin dang dor yang slar yang sbyin pa dag ni gza' dang chu tshod rkang par rnams su ste/
7a3(rgyud2): tshogs kyi cha la chu gter zla bas mnyam dang mi mnyam thob par dag la sbyin dor rkang pa'i don//stong la stong pa rnam dag bcu gsum zla ba'i rkang par lnga ste mig dang nyi ma dag la 
7a4(rgyud2): phyogs//bcu gcig me la tshes zhes bya ste bcu dang chu gter rkang pa nyi shu gcig gis dman pa'o//lnga dang bug par nyi shu rtsa gnyis dus dang nor la rgyal ba bdun par nyi shu rtsa 
7a5(rgyud2): lnga ste|de la nyi ma rab du dbye bas gsal byas nyi ma'i rkang pa dag ni sbyin dang dor bar 'gyur//'byung dang 'byung ba mda' dang rig byed me lag ri bong can ni snga ma'i cha dang gzhan la yang/
7a6(rgyud2): tshogs kyi cha la chu gterazla bas mnyam dang mi mnyamathob la rgyu ba rnams ni sbyin dang dor //rgyu zhing yod pas lhag ma bsgyur ba las ni dus dang nyi mas cha thob dag kyang de bzhin no//de 
7a7(rgyud2): nyid la ni nyi ma'i rab du dbye bas nyi ma'i rkang pa'i chu tshod sbyin dang dor bar 'gyur//gang zhig rig byed rnams kyis tshes bsgyur gsal bar nor te de yi sum cha dag gis bsres pa'o//
7b1(rgyud2): dus kyi rgyu skar rkang pa gsum bcas bu lon dag ste nyi ma'i longs spyod dbus nas sbyang bar bya//bya ba lhag ma mig dmar ji bzhin ro dus ri bong can rnams ngal ba'i las la rkang pa ste/
7b2(rgyud2): longs spyod kyis ni chu tshod bsgyur nas mda' dang yon tan zla bas cha thob longs spyod dang bcas//bgrod pa 'gro ba'i dbang gis nyi ma la ni sbyin dang dor te gzhan du dag la yod ma yin/
7b3(rgyud2): nyi ma la ni dus ro sbrul dang bcu dang 'phrog byed 'phrog byed drag po phyogs dang klu dang drug//ma deng nam mkha' dag dang bgrod pa'i mtha' te byang dang lho ru nor dang bu lon dag tu 'gyur/
7b4(rgyud2): zla ba 'pho ba'i dbye ba dag las phyogs kyi dbye bas zla ba drug ni nyin mor byed par spyod//nyi ma la byin tshes grangs dag kyang 'og tu ro yis bsgyur ba gza' yis longs spyod bsres pa 
7b5(rgyud2): dag||dman par gyur pa ri bong mtshan mar 'gyur te mi yi bdag po nyi zla bsres pa sbyor ba'o//byed pa dag ni tshes la mig gis bsgyur ba zla bral gang zhig bdun gyi cha yi lhag ma'o|
7b6(rgyud2): dag pa'i lo la klu bsres mkha' dang nam mkha' chu gter gyis bsgyur klu bsres rnams kyis sbyangs pa dag//ri zla me yis rab du phye ste nam mkha' ros bsgyur ri la sogs pas bgos pa 
7b7(rgyud2): chu tshod te//gnyis dang sum bcu'i gza' dang chu tshod nges pa 'dir lhan zla ba dag la lo yi pho ba'o//lugs sogs rnams kyis snon byed dag la gza' dang chu tshod rim pas yongs bkod 
8a1(rgyud2): @#/ O /bcu gnyis 'di dag ste//zla ba ri 'byung rig byed dag dang yon tan 'da' ste ri bong can dang ri zla gsum pa la'o//rig byed me 'byung zla ba 
8a2(rgyud2): mig dang lag pa dus dang rig byed mig ni drug pa dag la'o//drug dang zla ba mda' dang zla ba ri dus mdeng drug zla rig byed ri dang 'jig rten dang //drug dang rig byed stong pa dag ste stong pa 
8a3(rgyud2): mig mda' nyi ma'i go 'phang nya yi khyim la'o//zla drug 'phel dang 'grib par 'gyur te nyin dang mtshan mo dag kyang nyi ma rab du rgyu bas so//chu gter srog dang chu srang me ni nyin zhag 
8a4(rgyud2): so so'i dus su 'phel 'grib bgrod pa'i yan lag gis//lho phyogs su ni mtshan mo dag kyang 'phel bar 'gyur te kha ba'i ri'i byang du nyin me'o//zla ba dag la mig dang nyi ma bsres pa 
8a5(rgyud2): mig gis rnam bsgyur zla ba'i tshigs la gcig bsres te//mkha' dus chu yis rab phye thub pa lag pas bsgyur ba las kyang mkha' sogs cha yis skar ma'o//lhag ma drug bcus 
8a6(rgyud2): bsgyur ba gang yin de las chu tshod slar yang de bsgyur ba las chu bsrang ste//thob pas sman pa'i 'khor (la)gdong du 'gyur te mun can 'jug ma dag kyang rgyu skar phyed bsres pa'o//zla 
8a7(rgyud2): ba sum bcus bsgyur ba nyin zhag so so dag dang bcas pa las ni ri mkha' ris thob pa//gnas gsum dbus su rnam par bsres pa las kyang chus gter ros thob dman pa mi  
8b1(rgyud2): gsal nyin zhag go//ri dus gcig gis bsres pa dag kyang gsal ba'i nyin zhag tshogs te ri klu dus kyi dgos pa yi//lhag ma skar mas bsgyur thob slar yang nam mkha' ro yi   
8b2(rgyud2): bsgyur las ri la (sogs)gos pas chu tshod do//mig dmar la ni phyed btsas dgu sbyang gal te bu lon lhag na 'khor lo bsres la rnam par sbyangs//phyed pas lhag par gyur na'ang 'khor lo   
8b3(rgyud2): phyed ni dor bar bya ste dor ba'i lhag ma rig min 'gyur//skar ma drug bcus sgyur te chu tshod dag dang rab du ldan par gyur pa 'byung me zla bas so//gang thob dal ba'i las dag la yang nor 
8b4(rgyud2): dang bu lon dag ni rim pa min dang rim pas so//de nyid bcwo brgyad ri ni rab du gsal bar ro rkang 'khor lo phyed la steng dang 'og tu ste//longs spyod kyis ni chu tshod bsgyur las mda' dang yon 
8b5(rgyud2): tan zla bas 'khor lo phyed las cha thob pa//snga ma phyed la bzung byar 'dod de rkang pa'i dbang gis de bzhin phyi ma phyed la dor byar 'gyur //zos pa tshogs gcig byas nas gza' yi 
8b6(rgyud2): bgrod pa'i pa'i dbang gis sbyin dang dor ba dag kyang chu tshod la'o//de nas nyi mar sa skyes rnam par sbyangs te bla ma nyid skyes dag kyang myur ba'i las la'o//zla skyes dkar po la yang nyi  
8b7(rgyud2): ma dag ste myur ba'i las la nges par rims pas nor du 'gyur//sbyangs pa'i lhag mas 'dir ni rkang pa dag ste gza' rgyu rkang pa rnams kyis lhag ma'i chu tshod bsgyur//drug bcu'i 
10a1(rgyud2): cha yis thob pa gza' rgyu rkang pa dag las bzung bya dang ni dbrog cha de bzhin nyid//zos pa dag ni tshogs gcig byas nas gza' yi bgrod pa'i dbang gis 
10a2(rgyud2): sbyin dang dor ba mtha' dag ste//sa skyes la ni rkang pa rgyal ba sogs te rab gsal ma nu'i gnas la mtha' dag bzhag par bya//de ltar gza' rnams thams cad kyi ni rim par   
10a3(rgyud2): yongs su brtsi bar rig par bya'o mi'i dbang //zla skyes dkar po las ni nyin mor byed pas rnam dag zla skyes dkar po dag tu yang dag 'gyur//stong dang nam mkha' zla bas bsgyur 
10a4(rgyud2): ba'i mtha' dag nyin zhag tshogs la me dang nyi ma ri yis mnan//ri nas bug pa ri dang klu yis bgos te slar yang lhag ma skar ma la sogs dbye bas bsgyur //rab brjod cha yis
10a5(rgyud2): rgyu skar dag dang slar yang chu tshod rnams ni zla skyes longs spyod dag tu 'gyur //zla skyes dor te nyi ma'i dbus nas mi bdag bzang po mi bdag skar ma phyed kyang yang dag spang //lhag 
10a6(rgyud2): ma'i bya ba nor dang bu lon ngal ba myur ba'i las kyang mig dmar ji lta bzhin du 'gyur //zla skyes rkang pa dal ba'i las la bcu ri me ste myur ba'i las la mi bdag la sogs so//nyin 
10a7(rgyud2): zhag tshogs ni mkha' mkha' ro dang mig gis dman pa byas nas so me chu gter dag gis dgo//lhag ma skar mas bsnun las skar ma slar yang chu tshod bla ma yi yang ngal ba'i las
10b1(rgyud2): la'o//rgyu skar nyi ma sbyang ste 'phrog byed dgu dang rtsed mo can ni dal ba'i las la rkang pa'o//slar yang myur ba dag la rkang pa rab gsal ma nu'i gnas la phyogs rkang la sogs de dag 
10b2(rgyud2): go//nyin zhag mkha' dang zla bas bsgyur nas chu gter nor gyis dman pa ri dang rgyal ba mig gis bgo//skar mas sgyur ba las kyang skar ma'i longs spyod slar yang chu tshod myur ba'i 
10b3(rgyud2): las la pa sangs kyi//rgyu skar dus ni nyi ma sbyangs mda' dus ri bong can ni dal ba'i las la rkyang pa ste//myur la de nyid la sogs dbye bas rab gsal ma nu'i gnas dag la ste sbyin dor 
10b4(rgyud2): mig dmar bzhin//mkha' mig klu dang rig byed kyis dman gsal ba'i nyin zhag tshogs ni drug re ri mkha' zla ba yis//bgo zhing skar mas bsgyur las skar mar 'gyur te spen pa 
10b5(rgyud2): yi ni dor bya dag kyang bcwo brgyad do//rkang pa nyi shu rtsa gnyis tshes dus rab gsal ro yi gnas la'o myur ba'i las la ro la sogs//myur dal rgyu ba dag kyang rim pas rkang pa bgrod 
10b6(rgyud2): pa la ste 'khyog po nyid ni rim min gyis//phyed snga ma dang phyed phyir mar yang nyi ma 'gro ba'i dbang gis gza' rnams kyi ni 'byung ba yang //sum bcu rtsa bdun lag pas bsgyur ba'i 
10b7(rgyud2): zla ba rnams kyis 'jug rings dag ni myur dang dal dang 'khyog por 'gyur//gang dag su zhig nyi ma'i longs spyod la ni rab zhugs gza' de dag kyang nges par 'gyur//nyi ma btang 
11a1(rgyud2): nas 'char bar 'gyur te de yi lam yang bgrod pa 'gro ba'i rnam par dbye bas so//g.yon pa'i lam la gnas pa gang yin de ni nyi ma 'gro 
11a2(rgyud2): ba'i dbang gis g.yon nas 'gro ba ste//g.yas la gnas pa dag ni g.yas kyis slar yang gsal bar 'dir ni phan tshun 'thab ste dgra yin phyir//rang gi zhing la yang dag gnas 
11a3(rgyud2): rnams gal te de la dgra dag gyur na nges par 'thab pa'o//skar ma la ste gzhan la 'thab pa min par nges shing 'dir ni phan tshun mtshungs pa dag tu ldan//gal te nyi ma yi ni g.yon 
11a4(rgyud2): du zla ba rab zhugs gyur cing 'byung ba yang ni g.yon pa yis//byang gi rwa ni mtho bar 'gyur te kha cig tu ni bgrod pa'i dbang gis lho ru g.yas pa yang //tshig 'tshams su yang sgra gcan 
11a5(rgyud2): gdong dang mnyam par 'gro ste mngon du phyogs par 'dzin pa kho na'o//g.yon du rgyu ba dag la g.yas min du ste nyi ma'i bgrod pa'i dbang gis g.yas la g.yas pa'i char//de ltar 
11a6(rgyud2): sgra can gyis ni phyogs 'tshams su 'dzin ri bong 'dzin pa 'byung ba dag ni rgyab du yang //tha skar la sogs nag pa phyed du nyin dang mtshan mo mnyam pa'i dbus su 'go la'i ri mo'o//rgyu 
11a7(rgyud2): skar g.yas dang g.yas min dag la nges par srid pa'i gnas su khyim gyi dbye bas yang dag gnas//gzhu'i rnam pa'i dus khyim dag la mgo la'i dbus nas lhor ni me yi dkyil 
11b1(rgyud2): 'khor la gnas cing //byang du de ni gsil ba'i ri ste pad 'dab bzang po bzhin du shar du lug dang drang srogs la'o//me ni gsil ba'i ri'i 'tshams su dpag tshad stong phrag mkha' dang nam mkha' 
11b2(rgyud2): mkha' dang mda' ri'o//dbus su 'khor lo phyed kyi ri mo nyi ma'i bgrod pa dag kyang mtshan mo gcig ni mnyam pa la'o//phyi nas g.yas dang g.yas min dag tu rgyu ste nyin dang mtshan
11b3(rgyud2): mo rgyu skar dbye bas rim pa yis//lam rnams mkha' klu zla ba 'dor zhing nyin dang mtshan mo dag kyang ji srid bgrod pa dag gi mthar//nyi ma ri bong can gyi nyin mtshan chu tshod drug 
11b4(rgyud2): bcu dag las 'grib dang 'phel ba drug chas so//gang phyir ri la skye bo rnams kyi nyin mtshan mnyam par 'gyur te lug nyid srang gi nyi ma la'o//de phyir nyis brgyad dum bu dag la nges 
11b5(rgyud2): par glang la gnas dang sdig pa la gnas de bzhin no//bug pa zhes bya me grangs dag la 'khrig pa gzhur gnas phyogs kyi tshad dang bzhi pa dag la ni//nyi ma kar ka Ta la gnas pa 
11b6(rgyud2): dag dang chu srin la gnas lnga pa bcu gcig pa la yang //seng ge bum par rab zhugs bcu gnyis ro yi sar ni nya dang bu mor gnas pa dag la'o//'dir ni g.yon bgrod du 'gyur 
11b7(rgyud2): nyi ma chu srin gnas pa la ste bum par nyi mas gnyis pa la//nya la dum bu gsum par mi rnams kyi ni bgrod pa dag kyang lug gi nyi mas bzhi par ro//'byung ba zhes byar nyi ma

12a3(rgyud2): dag kyang bcu gnyis rtsibs kyi sa la'o//de bzhin lho bgrod du 'gyur nyi ma'i bgrod pa'i dbang gis kar ka Ta sogs khyim rnams dag la'o//steng du drug dang klu yi drangs ni

12a6(rgyud2): do||lan tshwa'i rgya mtsho 'gengs nas nyin mor byed pa dag ni lho ru me yi bar du 'gro bar byed//kai la shi' byang du 'gro ste gangs kyi ri la byang du yang ni byang la gnas pa'o//
12a7(rgyud2): mda' rnams tshes kyis bsgyur ba'i dpag tshad stong phrag rnams ni phyogs kyis bgrod pa dag la 'gro bar byed //phyed bcas mig slu bzlas thob nyin dang nyin la 'dor ba lnga dang

12b2(rgyud2): 'gyur te sa bzhi bcu gnyis rtsibs rnams la ni rims pas so//tshad gang dum bu gang du nyin mo'i dbang gis yin pa de ni bdun pa dag tu mtshan mo la'o//lam phyed 'bum phrag 
12b3(rgyud2): gsum po dag la nyi ma'i mdun du nyin mo dang ni rgyab du mtshan mo'o//ri'i rgyal po dag ni bskye bo thams cad kyi yang byang na gnas par 'gyur ro mi yi bdag//nyi ma 'tshar ba shar du   
12b4(rgyud2): 'gyur te ri dang mnyam pa bgrod pa dag dang de ni nub pa nub//dus dang thun 'tshams bzhi dang thun tshod nyid nyin dang mtshan mo dus kun du ni yang dag rgyu ba rnams//dus sbyor dbye bas phye 
12b5(rgyud2): bar gyur pa lhun po'i phyogs rnams su ni rab gsal nyi ma'i sa la 'khor //chu tshod dus la dpag tshad stong phrag drug ni rkang pa dang bcas nyin mor byed pa 'gro ba ste//drug bcu la ni 
12b6(rgyud2): me 'bum dag dang lnga riDa bo'i stong phrag nyin dang mtshan mo dag tu 'khor //nyin dang mtshan mor 'go la phyed la mkha' me chu tshod bgrod de mgo la dag la g.yas dang ni//g.yas min

13a1(rgyud2): @#//zung dag la yang mkha' mkha' gnyis te mda' ro'i chu tshod du 'gyur ku la'i ra dang seng ge la'o//bu mo la ni phyed bcas drug ste nges par srang 
13a2(rgyud2): sogs rim pa min pas de bzhin thams cad 'gyur//dus sbyor dag gis de nas gang phyir 'char ba dag kyang de bzhin nyi ma'i rgyu skar dbye bas so//stong phrag nyi shu rtsa gcig ro brgya ldan 
13a3(rgyud2): pa dag las so'i cha yis nges par phyung gyur pa//lus la dbugs lhag pa/dag nyin zhag so so'i dus su 'byung ba ri dang dus rnams dag gi grangs//stong phrag sum bcu rtsa drug dag

13a5(rgyud2): bgrod pa dag dang sa skyes zla ba dgu rnams dag gi mtshan mo//bla ma'i mdog tu 'gyur ba sangs kyi yang bkod par 'gyur te zla skyes kyi yang bgrod pa'o//tshes zhes bya ba'i lo ni nyi

13b1(rgyud2): lha mi lag 'gro rnams ni de nas lnga pa nyid ('gyur) zla ba phyed btsas mtshan mo dag gis kyang //grangs ni shin tu nges pa 'di dag dus kyi 'khor lor rab du grags pa yin no mi yi dbang //
13b2(rgyud2): ma bcad dus kyi 'khor lo'i bgrod pa dag la bu lon dgu ni bsres par gyur pa mtha' dag rnams//bkri shis la sogs gza' ni lnga po rnams kyi myur ba'i las dag la yang rkang pa'i 
13b3(rgyud2): chu tshod ste//sa skyes la ni rig byed dus mer rab 'gyur srid pa'i gnas rnams dag la rgyu ba'i dbye ba yis//snga phyi phyed la mtshungs par bu lon nor gyis bgrod pa dag la gnas kyi 
13b4(rgyud2): dbye bas nges par sbyar//dang por rgyal dbang grangs kyi chu tshod dag ste me dang mig ni rnam gsum dag tu rab du 'gyur//gnas gnyis dag la gcig dang mig ste bco brgyad tshes dang   
13b5(rgyud2): drag po me dang rtsa bar drag po ma dang //sum bcu rtsa brgyad (brgya)bcu dag dang brten pa srid pa'i gnas la 'dir ni gsum bcas lnga bcu'o//de yi nyi ma rnam dag 'gyur la bu lon dag dang nor 
13b6(rgyud2): yang rim pa min dang rim pas so//rig byed dag dang bug pa zla ba'i grangs ni gza' lag myur ba'i las la chu tshod dag tu 'gyur//gnas gnyis las ni bcu drug gzhan la tshes 
13b7(rgyud2): dang ma nu myos byed drag po ri dang dbang po mkha'//rtsa ba dag las rig byed drag (po)dong ni rab gsal nges par bcu gnyis pa la nyi shu ste//nyi shu brgyad dang sum bcu rtsa bzhi dag kyang  
14a1(rgyud2): ma nu'i gnas la rgyu ba'i dbye bas yang dag gnas//chu gter nam mkha' dag dang zla ba'i grangs te 'di dag rnams ni bla ma'i chu tshod tshogs su 'gyur/
